### RULES-18 - The prose rules assume a readable column, but no rule sets one; live copy runs about 123 characters per line
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 02 'Paragraph length' L141-143: 'one to three sentences per paragraph'. 03 §22 L353: 'Mostly 12-24 words per sentence'. Measured live: prose width 1,233px at 20px/36px ≈ 123 characters per line, from ArticleContent.tsx L6 `max-w-max prose ... lg:prose-xl` (LegacyPostContent L27 is the same). A 40-word, 2-sentence paragraph written to 02 renders as roughly 2 very long lines. No reference file mentions measure, line length, or column width.
- **Why it matters:** The short-paragraph voice rules only read well at about 60-80 characters per line. At 123 the page reads as a run of long single lines, which is harder to read however good the prose is.
- **Fix:** In 19 §10 'Reading design': 'Body prose measure 60-75ch (Tailwind `max-w-prose` or `max-w-[68ch]`); cards and tables may run wider; body 18px desktop/16px mobile, line-height 1.6-1.7.' Add to 18 §11: '[ ] Body prose measures ≤ 80 characters per line at 1280px.' Code ticket: replace `max-w-max` with a measure-bound wrapper in ArticleContent.tsx and LegacyPostContent.tsx and let full-width blocks (tables, card grids) break out.
- **Checker's correction:** The LegacyPostContent wrapper is at L12, not L27.

### RULES-19 - On mobile the product card shows the Verdict before the product name and Summary, against the contract's field order
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** Epilators handoff L101: 'Render each CTA after Cons, never before the Summary or Verdict'. The contract's visible order is Title, Editorial badge, Summary, Verdict, Pros, Cons, CTA. ProductReviewCard.tsx L35-69 puts image + rank + the Verdict box ('The Verdict' label, Playfair 2xl italic, in “ ” quotes) in the first column. Badge, H2 title and summary are in the second column (L71-86). With `flex-col` below lg, the Verdict appears before the product name.
- **Why it matters:** Phone readers (375px) get a quoted conclusion before they know which product it's about. Quoting the site's own verdict also makes editorial judgement look like a customer quote.
- **Fix:** In 19 §5 'Product review card', fix the reading order on all breakpoints: rank + image → badge → H2 name → Summary → Verdict (labelled box, no quotation marks) → Pros/Cons → CTA. Code ticket: move the verdict block into the second column after the summary, or use `order-*` classes below lg, and drop the “ ” wrapper. Add 'card reading order at 375px' to the live QA list.
- **Checker's correction:** The verdict is rendered in an actual <blockquote> element (L50), not only inside quote marks, which strengthens the 'reads as a quotation' point. The lines are L44-55 and L58-73, not L35-69 and L71-86.

### RULES-20 - The vault's callout vocabulary has no mapping to the single CMS callout type, and markdown callouts render as italic quotes
**medium** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** 16 L122-131 and 18 L69-73 define decision boxes, comparison callouts, mini tips, quick summaries and 'who should buy'. The epilators article writes its 'Type shortcut' callout as a markdown blockquote (article.md L217). The CMS has one `callout` object: title + string bullets + tone primary/secondary/neutral (objects/callout.ts), rendered with check-mark icons and an H3 title (CalloutCard.tsx L330-345). A body blockquote renders italic with a left border (article-portable-text.tsx L109-113). The guide item `tip` renders as 'EXPERT TIP' (GuideItemCard.tsx L18-26). Hedge publisher-cms-guide L47 says 'Convert <callout> blocks into the site's accessible callout', but no reference file says how.
- **Why it matters:** A decision rule entered as a blockquote reads as a quotation. A warning entered as a callout shows green check marks. Devices meant to make decisions easier end up visually misleading.
- **Fix:** In 19 §3/§5 add a 'Callout mapping' table. Decision box → calloutGroup, 1 callout, title 'Quick decision', 2-3 if/then bullets ≤ 20 words, tone primary. Comparison callout → calloutGroup, title 'A or B?', 2 bullets 'Choose A if… / Choose B if…', tone secondary. Mini tip → guideItem.tip (label changed to 'Quick check'), one sentence. Warning → calloutGroup tone neutral with a warning icon (code ticket: an icon per tone, not always Check). Ban markdown `>` blockquotes for anything that is not a real attributed quote. Add to 03 §12: 'Blockquote is reserved for attributed quotations.'
- **Checker's correction:** The CalloutCard lines are L59-73; the file has 79 lines, not L330-345.

### RULES-21 - No rule says how prices are shown, though several rules assume prices appear
**medium** · effort S · likely

- **Evidence:** 03 §8 L117 'Add a date to prices and discounts'. 12 L1031 'Prices and deals include a check date where shown'. 18 L66 requires cheap/mid/premium choices. 12 L945 'Do not add an Offer from a retailer snapshot that the site cannot keep current.' In practice, product.ts L108-115 stores `price` as an 'Amazon at import time' string that no component renders. The approved epilators copy says 'Premium price' and 'lower cost' with no tier or figure (article.md L36, L62), and the reader can't see a price band anywhere on the page. Every CTA reads 'Check Price'.
- **Why it matters:** Budget is one of the main criteria for choosing an epilator, but readers can't compare price levels on the page. Writers also don't know whether they may write a dollar figure.
- **Fix:** Add a 'Price display' section to 12 Part 8 and 19 §5. 'No numeric prices in article copy, cards, or tables. Show a price band per product in the comparison table ("Budget", "Mid-range", "Premium"), defined in the article contract from dated research observations, e.g. `price_bands: {budget: "< $50", mid: "$50-$120", premium: "> $120", observed: 2026-07-20}`, and recheck at each Stage 15 refresh. Numeric prices appear only if a live price feed with a visible timestamp is built.' Before adopting a numeric-price option, check the current Amazon Associates program policies on price display.
- **Checker's correction:** The Amazon price-display policy point is still unverified, as the finder noted.

### RULES-22 - Award labels have no length, brand, or trademark rules, and the epilators labels show why that matters
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 06 §7 (L264-289) and 12 Part 8 (L605-623) give good/weak examples only. Approved epilators awards include 'Best Streamlined ProGuide Kit' (a Philips trademark), 'Best Wet/Dry Braun Value' and 'Best Budget Braun Kit' (brand names in the award) (article-contract.md 'Approved ranking'). The badge renders as an 11px uppercase pill with 0.15em tracking (ProductReviewCard.tsx L73-76). On live, the same product carries different badge text in the table and in the card.
- **Why it matters:** Brand-scoped awards ('Braun value') suggest the ranking is inside one brand rather than across the field. Long labels wrap badly in the pill. Free-text labels in two places drift apart.
- **Fix:** Add to 06 §7: 'Award labels: 2-4 words, ≤ 28 characters; pattern "Best overall", "Best for <need>", "Best <attribute>"; no brand names, trademarks, or model series; each maps to one scored criterion named in the ranking record; entered once in articleProduct.tagline and reused by top picks and table.' Keep a site-wide label vocabulary list in 06 §7 (Best overall, Best value, Best budget, Best for sensitive skin, Best wet/dry, Best corded, Best for travel, Best full kit, Best for face ...) and require a contract note for any new label.
- **Checker's correction:** The badge pill is at ProductReviewCard.tsx L61, not L73-76.

### RULES-23 - FAQ rules don't set answer length or format, and the CMS default for FAQ schema contradicts policy
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 03 §21 and 12 Part 12 L846-854 say 'Answer directly in the first sentence', 'complete but concise', with no length. Approved epilators FAQ answers are 50-75 words, and one runs 5 sentences (article.md L321). The FAQ renders as collapsed `<details>` (Faq.tsx L255). The answer is Portable Text (faq.ts L15-18), so editors can enter several paragraphs and empty blocks, and those go straight into FAQPage JSON-LD via portableTextToHTML (lib/schema/post.ts L87-99; live Answer.text includes `<p><br/></p>`). sectionBlocks.ts L192-197 defaults `enable_schema: true`, while 12 L942 says 'Do not recommend FAQ schema merely because the article has an FAQ'. The handoff has no enable_schema field.
- **Why it matters:** FAQ answers are hidden until opened, so the first sentence carries the whole answer. Loose formatting leaks into structured data, and the schema is turned on by a CMS default rather than an editorial decision.
- **Fix:** Add to 03 §21: 'FAQ answer: one paragraph, 35-80 words; the first sentence (≤ 25 words) answers fully; no lists, no line breaks, no empty lines; 4-7 questions per roundup.' Add `faq_schema: false|true` with a reason to the handoff YAML (default false). In 19 §5, note that FAQ answers are collapsed and extracted into JSON-LD. Code ticket: default enable_schema false and strip empty blocks in the FAQ serializer.
- **Checker's correction:** The approved epilators FAQ has 6 answers of 41-79 words, not 50-75. The 5-sentence answer at L321 is confirmed. Faq.tsx is 23 lines, so the <details> element is at L7, not L255.

### RULES-24 - Image production has no owner, no size specs per slot, and a prompt that the rendering slot would crop
**medium** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** 17 step 8 and 18 L103 require a generation-ready prompt for roundup type visuals, but 17 L57 says 'Do not generate or publish images unless explicitly requested', and no stage produces the asset. The epilators handoff types prompt (L168) asks for 'landscape 16:9 ... four visually distinct cards from left to right', and says to place it 'beside the HTML type shortcut'. The CMS offers a full-width body iimage (rendered at a fixed 1280x720, article-portable-text.tsx L147-152) or a per-type `typeItem.image` rendered `aspect-square object-cover` (TypeItemCard.tsx L54-61), which would crop a 16:9 four-card graphic. The hero is `aspect-4/3 object-cover` (ArticleHero L157). No reference file gives aspect ratios or minimum sizes, and 12 Part 10 L754 'descriptive filename' can't be implemented on hash-named Sanity CDN URLs. The epilators live page has no type graphic.
- **Why it matters:** Image requirements are written into every package and never produced. When they are produced, the specs don't fit the slots, so decision graphics get cropped or skipped.
- **Fix:** Add an 'Image production' stage to 11 (between Stage 14 approval and Stage 16 publication) with an owner and a record: slot, source (exact-model licensed / generated diagram / stock), file, dimensions, alt, licence, reviewer. In 19 §6, fix slot specs: hero 4:3 at 1600x1200, safe area central 70%; OG image separately at 1200x630; product 1:1 at 1200x1200 on white; type card 1:1 at 800x800; in-body diagram 16:9 at 1600x900 as a full-width block, never 'beside'. Replace 'descriptive filename' in 12 Part 10 with 'upload with a descriptive original filename; the CMS vanity filename is a code ticket'. Amend 17 L57 to: 'Produce the prompts; image production is owned by the Image production stage.'
- **Checker's correction:** Image specs do exist, but only at cluster level: articles/hedge-trimmers/image-production-plan.md gives slot dimensions that match the frontend (hero 1280x960 4:3, OG 1200x630, product and type 1200x1200, inline 1280x720 16:9). The gap is that this was never promoted to reference/, not that no specs exist anywhere. The 16:9 epilators prompt fits the iimage slot. Only the 'beside the shortcut' placement cannot be built, and it would crop only if uploaded to typeItem.image.

### RULES-25 - The meta description is written as SERP copy but is also shown as the visible dek under the H1
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** ArticleHero.tsx L63: `const excerpt = post.seo?.meta_description ?? post.excerpt;`, rendered at 19px under the H1 (L81-85). The article schema has no excerpt field. 12 Part 4 L363-384 treats the meta description as a search-snippet tool ('Meta descriptions can be rewritten by search engines'). The epilators handoff has both `meta_description` ('Find the best epilator for your routine. Compare seven researched picks…') and `article_excerpt`, but only the former can render. The live dek is the legacy meta 'Looking for the best epilator?…'.
- **Why it matters:** A snippet written to earn a click becomes the first sentence the reader sees, repeating the title's intent ('Looking for the best epilator?') instead of starting the answer.
- **Fix:** Add to 12 Part 4: 'The meta description doubles as the on-page dek until a separate `dek` field exists. Write it as a plain statement a reader on the page would find useful, with no question hooks, 140-160 characters, full stops rather than colons.' Add `dek` to the 19 §2 page anatomy and open a code ticket for an `excerpt`/`dek` field on the article schema, with ArticleHero preferring it.

### RULES-27 - The rules ban identical product templates but set fixed fields and word windows that produce identical cards
**medium** · effort M · likely

- **Evidence:** 05 §10 'Identical product paragraph templates', 11 L397 'Identical product templates', and 16 L94 ('Do not begin every product card with the product name, award, or "This."') all push for variety. But 15 L124-125 fixes Summary 50-60 and Verdict 25-30 words, the contract bans extra paragraphs (`extra_product_paragraphs: false`), and audit_article.py L57-60 flags any deviation. ProductReviewCard renders every product with the same rank, image, quoted verdict, pill, H2, summary, 'Why we love it' / 'Points to consider' and CTA. The seven epilators summaries are 55-60 words each with parallel structure (article.md L50-176).
- **Why it matters:** Readers notice sameness at the layout level first. Varying word choice can't counter a card that is visually and rhythmically identical seven times.
- **Fix:** In 15 'Product cards', allow controlled variation: Summary 40-70 words; Verdict 18-35 words; 2-4 pros and 1-3 cons; plus two optional contract-approved fields rendered only when filled: `best_for` (≤ 8 words) and `skip_if` (≤ 12 words). Also allow one `comparison callout` between adjacent close rivals (e.g. BRE708/00 vs BRE728/00). Update audit_article.py windows to match. In 19 §5, document the optional card rows so the component ticket can add them.
- **Checker's correction:** The epilators summaries are 50-56 words (54, 54, 52, 50, 53, 56, 55), not 55-60. The verdicts are 25-27 words.

### RULES-28 - Template-added sections (Related posts, newsletter) and link targets outside the vault aren't governed, and cluster sync only inventories vault packages
**medium** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** page.tsx L96-98 appends an automatic `RelatedPosts` by category. The live epilators page shows an H2 'Related posts' with six 2023-2024 legacy titles, one with the typo 'T0'. No reference file mentions this module, although 03 §18 and 12 Part 9 govern internal links. The approved pillar links to /skin-care/can-you-use-an-epilator-on-pubic-hair/ and /skin-care/how-to-epilate-underarms/ (article.md L243, L255), neither of which is in the 223-URL sitemap. The handoff marks them 'publish_with_support_page', but no rule blocks publishing the pillar alone. final-cluster-sync-2026-07-20.md's inventory lists 4 vault pages but not the live /skin-care/braun-epilator/, which competes for the same keyword space. 17 step 2 checks keyword ownership only among vault packages.
- **Why it matters:** The last links on the page point readers to stale content chosen by an algorithm. Publishing the pillar before its support pages would create 404 links, and cannibalisation by legacy URLs goes unchecked.
- **Fix:** (1) In 19 §2, name Related posts as template-owned, and add a handoff field `related_articles: [3 slugs]`. Code ticket: curated override with category fallback excluding posts older than N months. (2) Add to 17 step 1: 'Inventory must include every live sitemap URL in the category whose slug or title matches the cluster topic; assign each an owner action (keep, merge + 301, refresh).' (3) Add to 11 Stage 16: 'A page may publish only when every internal link target returns 200; otherwise remove the link or publish the target first (atomic cluster release).'
- **Checker's correction:** 17 step 10 (L40) already requires every link to 'resolve to the intended live page', but it runs before publication, and the handoff's 'publish_with_support_page' status defers it. What is missing is a check at publication time. RelatedPosts is at page.tsx L95 and L101.

### RULES-V01 - The documented hero-image destination doesn't exist on the article model, and a separate social image would replace the hero
**medium** · effort S · verified · found by the checker

- **Evidence:** articles/hedge-trimmers/image-production-plan.md L15-16 and L212 say to 'Upload each 1280 x 960 px hero to the article `thumbnail` field' and to put an optional 1200x630 social crop in `seo.open_graph_image`. sanity/schemas/article/index.ts has no thumbnail field (its fields are title, slug, article_format, editorial_badge, seo, author, categories, publishedAt, content, products). src/lib/post-seo.ts getPostPrimaryImage returns getImageUrl(post.seo?.open_graph_image) ?? getImageUrl(post.thumbnail), and ArticleHero.tsx L157-159 renders that image in an aspect-4/3 object-cover frame. So on article-model pages the OG image IS the hero. The measured facts confirm og:image = the hero on epilators (1448x1086), with og:image:width/height hard-coded to 1280x720.
- **Why it matters:** A publisher who follows the only written image spec can't find the thumbnail field. If they then supply the recommended 1.91:1 social image, it becomes the 4:3 hero and gets cropped. The social card also gets wrong dimension metadata. Nothing in reference/ records this rule, so it stays invisible.
- **Fix:** In the proposed 19-article-design-and-rendering-spec.md §6, state: 'Article-model hero = seo.open_graph_image, 4:3 at 1600x1200 or larger. There is no separate social image until a heroImage field exists.' Correct image-production-plan.md L15-16, L29, L68 and L212 to match. Code ticket: add a `heroImage` (iimage with required alt) field to sanity/schemas/article/index.ts, make getPostPrimaryImage prefer heroImage for the hero and open_graph_image for og:image, and derive og:image:width/height from the asset metadata instead of hard-coding 1280x720.

### RULES-V02 - The cluster-sync gate holds the commercial pillar until the slowest support page, including medical review, has cleared
**medium** · effort S · likely · found by the checker

- **Evidence:** README L75: 'Run 17 only after every included article has passed its own ... checks and reached the required manual editorial gate'. 11 Stage 14 L518: run 17 'after every page has completed its individual audit and before CMS publication'. 15 L192 says the same. final-cluster-sync-2026-07-20.md L13-18 shows the two support pages at 'Ready for medical and editorial review'. The epilators handoff internal-link statuses read 'publish_with_support_page'. The pillar has been ready_for_editorial_review since 2026-07-20 and is still unpublished (measured facts).
- **Why it matters:** The rules make the pillar's publication wait on the pages that need the most review, medical review of the pubic-hair and underarm guides, while the ranking legacy URL keeps serving contradictory claims. This is inference about the cause of the 67-day delay, but the rules do create that dependency.
- **Fix:** Add to 11 Stage 14 and 17 Purpose: 'Cluster sync can run on the ready subset. A pillar may publish before its support pages. Links to unpublished support pages are removed or held in the handoff as `deferred_link` and added when the target goes live. Then rerun 17 step 10 for the new pages.' Record the per-page publication order in the cluster-sync output (new section K: 'Release order and deferred links').

### RULES-15 - Stage, gate, and route names drift across 11, 13, 14 and 15, and 13 contradicts the README's roundup route
**low** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 'Gate 6' is 'criteria must match intent' in 11 (L243) but 'Final audit and publication approved' in 13 (L1310). The Final Article Audit is 11 Stage 13, 13 Step 18, 14 Prompt 36 (§38) and 15 Part 4. 13 §4.1 (L1177-1199) says a product roundup should 'Use every phase' (19 steps), but README L49-51 and 15 L196 say the normal roundup uses the four-part profile. 13 §6 L1338 says the minimum workflow 'is not sufficient for a major product roundup', while 15 L196 says 'Use the four-part workflow by default for a major roundup'. Refresh cadence appears only in 13 Step 21 (L1147-1160). 11 Stage 15 says 'At the scheduled review' with no cadence, and 06 §17 refers to 'the project's update interval', which is never defined in a normative file.
- **Why it matters:** An agent following 13 runs the 19-step path on a normal roundup. Gate references in audits ('Gate 6 passed') are ambiguous, and refresh intervals are never actually scheduled.
- **Fix:** (1) Adopt one naming scheme: '11 Stage N' is canonical. In 13 and 14, prefix each step/prompt heading with its stage, e.g. '## Step 18 (11 Stage 13): Final Article Audit'. Rename 13's 'Gate 1-6' to 'Handbook checkpoint A-F'. (2) Change 13 §4.1's first line to 'Use this full sequence only for the high-risk route in README; normal roundups use 15.' Change 13 §6 L1338 to 'not sufficient for a product roundup; use 15'. (3) Move the cadence list from 13 Step 21 into 11 Stage 15 as normative, and replace 06 §17's 'project's update interval' with 'the cadence in 11 Stage 15'.
- **Checker's correction:** 13's own Purpose section (L5) already says 'For normal product roundups handled by one agent, use 15 as the default four-part path', so 13 does not contradict the README route overall. The conflict is limited to the §4.1 and §6 wording read out of context. Severity lowered to low. The §6 line is L1340.

### RULES-26 - Hedge-trimmer specifics have leaked into the generic roundup profile, style rules, and the audit script
**low** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 15 'Buying guide' typical factors (L140-147): 'Corded versus cordless layout', 'Blade length and cut capacity', 'Battery and charger cost'. These appear in a profile used for epilators, bug zappers and skin care. Move the hedge list into articles/hedge-trimmers/content-strategy.md. In 03 §22, change the examples to category-neutral wording ('hyphenate compound measurements before nouns, e.g. 40-minute runtime'). In audit_article.py, load US_TERMS and compound-modifier patterns from an optional per-category config (e.g. `--category garden`).

### RULES-29 - There's no title-tag separator or brand-suffix rule, so handoffs mix colons, pipes, and no suffix
**low** · effort S · likely

- **Evidence:** Handoff seo_title values: 'Best Epilator in 2026: 7 Picks for At-Home Hair Removal' (epilators), '5 Best Corded Hedge Trimmers for 2026 | WhoAdvice' (hedge corded), 'How to Epilate Underarms Safely: Step-by-Step Guide'. The live epilators title has no brand; live hedge has '| WhoAdvice'. src/lib/post-seo.ts L43 uses `meta_title || title` with no suffix logic. 02 'Headline tone' and 12 Part 4 examples encourage 'X vs. Y: …' colon patterns. Arif's recorded default across his sites is ' - ' separators and no colons in title or meta (memory 'no-colons-in-head-fields', 2026-09-23, written for SaveMP3 and 'by default for the other sites').
- **Why it matters:** Inconsistent SERP titles weaken brand recognition, and they conflict with the owner's stated preference for head fields.
- **Fix:** Add to 12 Part 4: 'Title tag = <page title> - WhoAdvice when it fits ≤ 60 characters, otherwise omit the suffix; separator " - "; no colons or pipes; meta description uses full stops, no colons.' Update the 02 headline patterns and 12 good examples to match, and fix existing handoffs at their next refresh. Confirm with Arif that the SaveMP3 ruling applies to WhoAdvice before changing live titles.

### RULES-30 - Handoff schemas and freshness fields drift between 12, 13 and the skill
**low** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 13 Step 19 package (L1037-1057) includes `article_title`, `short_excerpt`, `tags`, `featured_image_brief`, `affiliate_disclosure`, `methodology_note`, `last_fact_checked`, `next_review_date`. The 12 Part 14 YAML (L903-924) and skill deliverables.md (L68-89) have neither `last_fact_checked` nor `next_review_date`, and use `article_excerpt` / `recommended_tags` names. The epilators handoff has neither date field, so no refresh is scheduled for a page whose title includes '2026'.
- **Why it matters:** Without a recorded next-review date, Stage 15 never starts. The year in the title and the product availability go stale without anyone being prompted.
- **Fix:** Fold this into the schema-mapped handoff from RULES-04. Keep one YAML in 12 Part 14 including `last_fact_checked`, `next_review_date` (from the 11 Stage 15 cadence) and `title_year_valid_until`. Delete the 13 Step 19 YAML and replace it with a pointer, and make deliverables.md point to 12 instead of restating it.

### RULES-31 - The 64 KB prompt library isn't used by any package and repeats rules that then drift
**low** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** 14-article-prompt-library.md is 64,121 bytes, 47 sections and 36 prompts. grep across articles/ finds no package that references 14 or a 'Prompt N'. 15 L7 says it 'replaces the 17-prompt minimal sequence'. 14's product-section prompt (L1172-1181) lists fields without the 50-60/25-30 word windows in 15. Prompt 22 (L1291-1320) asks for two quick-pick versions plus a recommendation, which conflicts with the 15 contract's fixed top-pick fields.
- **Why it matters:** It adds load cost and a second place where rules drift, for no observed use.
- **Fix:** Move 14 to reference/archive/14-article-prompt-library.md. Keep in the live directory only §41 (informational), §42 (single review), §43 (comparison), §44 (editor revision) and §45 (update), as reference/14-prompt-recipes.md (about 10 KB), each starting 'Rules come from 03/12/15; this prompt adds only task framing'. Update README L40 and the SKILL.md routes (L17-20).

### RULES-32 - The deprecated pointer file and stray zero-byte files are vault clutter
**low** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** reference/on-page-seo-checklist.md (414 bytes) exists only for 'older article records'. The only reference to it is articles/hedge-trimmers/audits/on-page-seo-humanization-audit-2026-07-16.md L5. The vault root has zero-byte files `best`, `distinct`, `leading`, `material` (dated Jul 13 17:24), which look like shell redirection accidents. The hedge README's six-file folder contract lists `research.md`, but epilators packages keep Part 1 in articles/epilators/research/ instead.
- **Why it matters:** Minor, but it's noise every agent session loads or trips over, and the folder contract differs between clusters.

Arif should confirm and remove the four zero-byte root files. Add a 'Package folder contract' section to 11 Stage 4 naming the required files (article, article-contract, content-brief, research or research/ link, sources, audit, publisher-handoff, and after Stage 16 a publication-record.md), so every cluster follows the same layout.

### RULES-V03 - The 15 contract template still lists Slug and Brand as visible card fields, which the approved contracts and handoffs forbid
**low** · effort S · verified · found by the checker

- **Evidence:** 15-fast-roundup-workflow.md L50-58 sets product_card_fields: Title, Slug, Editorial badge, Brand, Summary, Verdict, Pros, Cons. The epilators article-contract.md L31-44 splits these into visible_product_card_fields (Title, badge, Summary, Verdict, Pros, Cons, CTA) and structured_product_record_fields (Slug, Brand, ASIN, links). The epilators publisher-handoff.md L28 says these fields 'must not appear as visible product-review rows'. Hedge publisher-cms-guide.md L53 and deliverables.md L43-46 still follow the old visible-field shape, and audit_article.py L70 requires Slug and Brand in every card.
- **Why it matters:** The controlling profile, the skill template and the validator describe a card format that the latest approved contract rejected. So each new package re-litigates the format, and the validator's card checks can never pass on the approved format (see RULES-11).
- **Fix:** Replace 15 L50-58 with two keys: `visible_product_card_fields: [Title (as heading), Editorial badge, Summary, Verdict, Pros, Cons, CTA]` and `structured_product_record_fields: [Slug, Brand, ASIN, raw_affiliate_url, affiliate_link]`. Update deliverables.md L40-64 and audit_article.py L70 (required_card_fields) to match, and remove the Slug and Brand lines from the markdown card template.

## Publishing pipeline

### PIPE-01 - No runnable importer exists. The hardened importer was deleted four hours after it was committed, and the only surviving copy is an older, unsafe, uncommitted script in the vault that cannot run where its own header says to run it
**critical** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** blog git: 9c80846 (2026-09-17 12:54) added scripts/import-digital-clocks-draft.ts (1,396 lines), scripts/lib/sanity-document-ids.ts, scripts/lib/sanity-import-safety.ts, scripts/migrate-legacy-sanity-ids.ts, docs/cms-import-runbook.md, `cms:import` package scripts and the @sanity/id-utils dependency. c754176 (16:29 the same day) deleted all of them ('remove legacy sanity document ID handling'). Today ~/personal/blog has no scripts/ directory, node_modules/.bin has no tsx, and node_modules/@sanity has no id-utils. The vault copy, ~/code/whoadvice/scripts/import-digital-clocks-draft.ts, shows as 'M' in git status (67+/11- uncommitted). Its lines 4-5 say 'Run from the WhoAdvice blog checkout: pnpm exec tsx scripts/import-digital-clocks-draft.ts'. The vault has no package.json or node_modules. AGENTS.md line 5 still requires '@sanity/id-utils' and line 8 requires 'Patch only importer-owned fields'. Nothing that exists implements either rule.
- **Why it matters:** Every approved package (4 epilator, 4 bug-zapper, 7 unpublished hedge-trimmer pages) has no automated route to Sanity. The written safety rules point at code that is gone. Anyone who 'just runs the importer' will run the unsafe vault version (see PIPE-02 and PIPE-03).
- **Fix:** Restore the 9c80846 files into the blog repo under scripts/cms/ (git show 9c80846:<path>). Add tsx and @sanity/id-utils as devDependencies and add package scripts `cms:import`, `cms:verify`, `cms:audit-ids`. Rename the importer to scripts/cms/import-article.ts. Replace the vault copy with a README pointer so there is only one importer. Put the importer in the blog repo's vitest config, with tests shown to fail against a deliberately broken fixture.
- **Checker's correction:** The vault copy is 1,177 lines. It was committed 22 minutes before the hardened blog copy (69bcd14 at 12:32 vs 9c80846 at 12:54). It is 'older' only in that sense; the uncommitted diff also carries later mainAuto work. AGENTS.md survived the deletion because c754176 did not touch it.

### PIPE-02 - The vault importer creates dotted document IDs (`article.<slug>`, `product.<slug>-<asin>`). The repo's own rules forbid these, and they are likely unreadable by the token-less live client
**high** · effort S · likely

- **Evidence:** vault importer line 703 `product.${slugify(product.title)}-${product.asin.toLowerCase()}`, line 821 `'article.best-digital-clocks'`, line 1044 `?? `article.${slug}``, line 1060 (the same product pattern). AGENTS.md line 5: 'Ordinary root IDs must be UUID-style IDs without dots'. sanity/lib/client.ts lines 12-15: the published `client` has no token. The deleted sanity-import-safety.ts (9c80846) says 'Legacy dotted IDs are migration inputs, never reusable public roots'. The deleted migrate-legacy-sanity-ids.ts existed only to clone `article.*`/`product.*` documents to UUID roots.
- **Why it matters:** Sanity treats an ID containing '.' as a sub-path. Such documents are not returned to unauthenticated requests. A dotted product would dereference to null on the live site, and resolveArticleProducts or resolveTopPickItems would then drop it silently (PIPE-08). The article could also be invisible. The existence of a migration script shows such documents were created at least once.
- **Fix:** Generate new root IDs with createPublicRootId() (a UUID via @sanity/uuid) and derive drafts with getDraftId from @sanity/id-utils, as the deleted sanity-document-ids.ts did. Run assertNoDraftOrVersionReferences on every payload before commit. Then run the read-only audit query from the deleted scripts/audit-legacy-sanity-ids.ts to list any dotted documents that already exist, and migrate them with the clone-without-delete script after a dataset export.
- **Checker's correction:** Downgraded from critical to high: it is a latent defect of a script that is not currently runnable (PIPE-01). There is also counter-evidence to weigh. The live /home-gadgets/best-digital-clocks/ article was almost certainly created by this importer's main() path: its H2 'Our seven digital-clock picks' and badge 'Research-Based Guide • 2026' match the hard-coded strings at lines 367 and 829. It renders anonymously (HTTP 200, datePublished 2026-09-16T19:17:41Z). So either those documents were already migrated to UUID roots, or the 'invisible to the token-less client' consequence did not apply to them. A read-only ID audit is still needed to settle it.

### PIPE-03 - The importer overwrites whole documents with createOrReplace, so a re-import erases editor edits, the hero image, publishedAt and any Studio-only fields
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** vault importer line 1170 `await client.createOrReplace(document)`, line 1168 `transaction.createOrReplace({ ...product, _id: ... })`, lines 907 and 918 (the same in main()). The article payload at line 1164 contains only title, slug, format, badge, seo{meta_title, meta_description, keywords, indexable}, author, categories, products and content. It has no seo.open_graph_image, no publishedAt and no hero. ArticleHero takes its hero from getPostPrimaryImage (src/lib/post-seo.ts lines 46-52: seo.open_graph_image, then thumbnail). AGENTS.md line 8: 'Patch only importer-owned fields. Preserve images, editorial fields, and unrelated manual changes.' The deleted 9c80846 importer used createIfNotExists(seed).patch(id, set(fields)) (lines 1067-1068, 1340-1344).
- **Why it matters:** A second dry-run-then-commit, for example after a price recheck, wipes the editor's Studio work and the hero/OG image. Publishing that draft then ships a hero-less page (the image column collapses) with no og:image. New products are also created published with no image (line 1168 writes to the root ID when the product is not yet published).
- **Fix:** Write with createIfNotExists({_id, _type}) plus patch.set() limited to an explicit allow-list of importer-owned paths (title, slug, seo.meta_title, seo.meta_description, products[_key==...] editorial fields, content). Use setIfMissing for author, categories and publishedAt. Never set seo.open_graph_image or product.image unless the payload carries a new asset and the field is empty. Pass ifRevisionId(<rev read in dry run>) so the commit aborts if an editor changed the draft after the dry run. Print a field-level diff against the existing draft or published document in dry-run mode.
- **Checker's correction:** Downgraded from critical to high, because the damage happens only on a re-import over an existing article, and no importer is runnable today. Detail: product writes spread the existing published and draft product fields (line 1062 `{...(publishedProduct ?? {}), ...(existingProduct ?? {}), ...}`), so an existing product's image survives. Only new products are created without an image, directly at the published root ID. The article loss is broader than stated: even the FIRST import over an existing published article builds drafts.<id> from scratch. Publishing that draft drops the published article's seo.open_graph_image (the hero), publishedAt and any Studio-only fields.

### PIPE-04 - The importer is hard-wired to digital clocks and epilators instead of reading publisher-handoff.md, which is why every other cluster is stuck
**high** · effort L · verified

- **Evidence:** main(): DEFAULT_ARTICLE_PATH (line 78-79) is digital-clocks. EXPECTED_ASINS and EXPECTED_AFFILIATE_LINKS (lines 81-99) are hard-coded. Section names are literals: 'Our seven digital-clock picks' (line 367), 'Three leading digital clocks', 'Frequently asked questions', 'Which digital clock should you choose?'. Counts are fixed at 7/3/7/6 (lines 556-567). The category is fixed as 'home-gadgets' (line 664) and the slug as 'best-digital-clocks' (line 817). The keyword list is fixed (lines 834-841). mainAuto(): EPILATOR_PRODUCT_OVERRIDES (lines 940-952) hard-codes 11 epilator ASINs and short links that already exist in epilators/best-epilators-at-home-hair-removal/publisher-handoff.md lines 30-95. inferCategory (lines 1005-1011) knows only '/epilators/' and '/hedge-trimmers/'. Section routing relies on heading regexes (/top|leading|three best/, /^Types of\b/, /^How to choose\b/, /^Frequently asked questions\b/). Nothing parses a handoff YAML block.
- **Why it matters:** Product identity now lives in two places, the handoff YAML and TypeScript constants, and they will drift. A bug-zapper or hedge-trimmer package whose buying guide is titled 'Buying guide' or whose top section is 'Our picks' silently drops into generic prose. The approved components (typesSection and guideSection cards) are then lost with no error.
- **Fix:** Define an importer contract keyed on publisher-handoff.md. Required YAML: recommended_slug, category, seo_title, h1, meta_description, article_excerpt, author_slug, editorial_badge, products[{rank, title, brand, slug, asin, affiliate_link, badge, image}], internal_links[{url, anchor, status}], featured_image{file, alt}, schema{article, itemList, productMarkup:false}. Let the article body declare component boundaries with explicit HTML comment markers that the vault already uses ('CMS TOP 3 PICKS COMPONENT', 'CMS IMAGE'), for example <!-- cms:top-picks -->, <!-- cms:comparison -->, <!-- cms:product-reviews -->, <!-- cms:types -->, <!-- cms:guide -->, <!-- cms:faq -->, instead of heading regexes. Remove EXPECTED_* and EPILATOR_PRODUCT_OVERRIDES. Make the dry run fail when a marker is present but its section does not parse.
- **Checker's correction:** 'which is why every other cluster is stuck' overstates the cause. The importer is one blocker. The others are the missing approval record (PIPE-13) and the deleted tooling (PIPE-01).

### PIPE-05 - The Markdown-to-Portable-Text conversion loses or corrupts approved content: callouts, blockquotes, numbered lists, section intros and heading text
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** Callouts, lines 1016-1017: only the FIRST <callout> is captured, re-titled 'What the evidence shows', and moved to the top of the article. The global regex `/<callout>...<\/callout>/gi` deletes every other callout, and the vault uses <callout> in do-bug-zappers-work and 5 hedge-trimmer articles. Blockquotes: markdownLinesToPortableText (lines ~473-528) handles only #-headings and '- ' bullets. The epilator callouts are blockquotes ('> **Type shortcut:**' at best-epilators article.md line 217, and the same form in face, pubic-hair and underarm pages), so they become paragraphs that literally start with '>'. The pubic-hair '> 1.' numbered list is not handled at all. The 'How to choose' branch (lines 1104-1118) drops any text before its first ###. The product section is replaced by a hard-coded H2 'Product reviews' (line 1087), overwriting the approved 'Best epilator reviews'. The top-picks section heading ('Top 3 epilator picks') is not emitted at all (line 1083).
- **Why it matters:** The hedge-trimmer publisher guide (lines 16, 47) forbids silently rewriting approved copy and headings. The importer does it mechanically, and the dry run does not report the loss.
- **Fix:** Parse with a real Markdown AST (remark-parse + remark-gfm) and convert mdast to Portable Text with a node-by-node mapper. Map blockquote-with-bold-lead and <callout> both to calloutGroup at their original position, using the bold lead as the title. Map ordered lists to listItem 'number' and emphasis to 'em'. Keep the approved H2 text for every component by emitting the heading block before the component. Throw on any unmapped node type. Add a round-trip check to the dry run: Portable Text to plain text must match article.md body text word-for-word, ignoring the metadata lines.

### PIPE-06 - Internal links are imported as raw URL strings with no referential integrity, whoadvice.com links are marked external, and links to 404 support pages would ship
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** inlineChildren (lines ~399-455) writes markDefs `{_type:'inlineLink', linkType:'url', url, is_external: /^https?:\/\//.test(url)}`. The epilator article links 'https://whoadvice.com/skin-care/best-face-epilators/' (line 239), so is_external=true, and src/lib/resolve-inline-link.ts lines 63-68 then render target=_blank rel='noopener noreferrer'. The schema supports linkType 'article' with a post/article reference (sanity/schemas/objects/inline-link.ts lines 46-60). The article also links /skin-care/can-you-use-an-epilator-on-pubic-hair/ and /skin-care/how-to-epilate-underarms/ (lines 243, 255). curl on 2026-09-25 returned 404 for both. The handoff marks them 'publish_with_support_page' (lines 116-121).
- **Why it matters:** Importing the pillar alone ships two broken internal links, and an internal link that opens a new tab. A future slug change breaks string links silently, and nobody can ask Sanity which pages link to a URL.
- **Fix:** In the importer, resolve every root-relative or whoadvice.com URL to its published post/article document by category and slug, then emit linkType 'article' with a reference (strip the host). Fail the dry run if the target is not published, unless handoff internal_links[].status is 'hold', in which case unwrap the link to plain text and list it in the report. Keep linkType 'url' with is_external=true only for other hosts. Add rel 'sponsored' automatically for amzn.to or amazon.com hosts in resolve-inline-link.ts.
- **Checker's correction:** The same failure has already shipped on a live page: see PIPE-V01 (the published cordless-hedge article links to a 404 support page).

### PIPE-07 - Products are matched only by ASIN. Legacy product documents (ASIN optional) are missed, so imports create duplicate products, publish them with no image, and orphan the old affiliate links
**high** · effort M · likely

- **Evidence:** vault importer line 1057 `*[_type == "product" && upper(asin) == $asin]`, and a new document when no match is found (line 1060). sanity/schemas/product.ts lines 40-41: ASIN 'Optional during migration'. The product image is not required (lines 117-122). The live epilator page links amzn.to/3S0hLmI, 43t5UQs, 446pNwM, 4eg9bro, 4oh7U7O, 4ok0WPw, 4v4L8mn and 4vGOPOU. None of these are among the handoff's seven links (4prmaeK, 4pw4HSG, 4gIcrP5, 4fmtFPE, 4b3MOEK, 4fa6R6V, 4fkA25V), although 3-270 and BRE227/00 appear on both. bug-zappers/audits/live-local-status-audit-2026-09-17.md lines 61-63: 'A raw-perspective Sanity query by their exact ASINs returned zero product documents' for 19 records.
- **Why it matters:** Each cluster import would create a second product document for models that already exist, for example Braun 3-270 and Philips BRE227/00. The old documents keep old links and appear in other articles. The new ones have no images, so cards render imageless. Two docs for one ASIN is exactly what the ASIN uniqueness validator (product.ts lines 51-73) cannot catch when the legacy doc has no ASIN.
- **Fix:** Add a separate, reviewable 'product dependency' phase before any article write. (1) Match by ASIN. (2) If nothing matches, fuzzy-match by brand plus normalized model token among products whose asin is not defined, and list the candidates in the dry-run report for a human 'reuse <id> | create' decision recorded in the handoff (products[].sanity_id). (3) On reuse, patch asin and link on the product draft. (4) Require products[].image in the handoff and upload it. (5) Publish products only after the report is approved (AGENTS.md line 4). Also make `image` required on product (Rule.required() with a warning during migration).
- **Checker's correction:** Downgraded from critical to high. Duplicate product documents are a data-integrity and maintenance cost, not a reader-facing error. The new article would still render its own new links. Overlapping models: 3-270, BRE227/00 and also SES9-041 (live as 'SES9-041 3D'), not just two. The ASIN duplicate validator (product.ts lines 51-73) also compares `asin == $asin` against an upper-cased parameter, so a lower-case stored ASIN escapes it too.

### PIPE-08 - Unresolved product references vanish silently at render, so a broken import publishes as a thinner page instead of failing
**high** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** src/lib/resolve-article-product.ts lines 53-67 and 134-144: an entry whose product did not dereference fails isArticleProductEntry and is dropped via flatMap. src/lib/resolve-top-pick-items.ts line 187: `block.items.filter((item) => item.product?.title)`. sanity/schemas/objects/article-product.ts line 22: `// LEGACY: restore Rule.required() after full content migration`, so an articleProduct can publish with no product.
- **Why it matters:** Combined with PIPE-02 and PIPE-07, a 7-product roundup can go live with 4 cards, a 2-card top-picks strip and a comparison table that references missing cards, and nothing surfaces it. This is also the likely mechanism behind the live mismatch between 8 table rows and 7 cards.
- **Fix:** Restore Rule.required() on articleProduct.product. Add article-level validation that every topPickItem.product and comparisonRow.product _ref is in article.products[].product._ref. In getArticle (src/lib/requests.ts line 39), count raw products against resolved products and console.error with the slug when they differ, so Vercel logs catch it. Make the live QA script (PIPE-20) assert card count == handoff products length.
- **Checker's correction:** Wrong line numbers: resolve-article-product.ts lines 31-45 and 112-122 (not 53-67/134-144); resolve-top-pick-items.ts line 21 (not 187). The claim that this is 'the likely mechanism behind the live mismatch between 8 table rows and 7 cards' is wrong. /skin-care/best-epilators/ is a legacy post, and its comparison_table rows reference products independently of post.products[]. Nothing validates that each table row's product is in products[], so the Ubroo IPL row exists without a card. That gap belongs in the fix (row product ⊆ products), but it is not a silent-drop case.

### PIPE-09 - The Studio 'Revalidate now' action and the post-migration revalidation always fail with HTTP 400
**high** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** sanity/actions/revalidateContent.tsx lines 52-56 call requestContentRevalidation({slug, categorySlug, type: 'content'}). sanity/lib/revalidateContent.ts line 7 defaults type = 'content'. src/app/api/revalidate/manual/route.ts lines 21-29 accept only article, post, category, author, homePage, siteLayout and config, and lines 76-86 return 400 'Unsupported revalidation type: content'. sanity/actions/migrateToArticle.tsx line 72 calls requestContentRevalidation({slug, categorySlug}) and swallows the error (lines 73-75).
- **Why it matters:** The only manual way to push a Studio change live is broken. Editors fall back to waiting for the webhook. The epilator page's X-Vercel-Cache HIT with Age ≈ 6 days (measured facts) is consistent with purges not happening.
- **Fix:** In sanity/actions/revalidateContent.tsx send `type: type as 'article'|'post'` for content documents. Change RevalidateParams.type to the route's union and delete the 'content' literal. Add a vitest case that POSTs the action's exact body to the manual route handler and expects 200. Show the error text in the Studio toast instead of window.alert.
- **Checker's correction:** Category and author revalidation from the same action still work. Only article/post ('content') fails. The X-Vercel-Cache Age≈6 days does not show that purges are failing: the page had no content change to purge. Drop that line from the evidence.

### PIPE-11 - The 'draft → preview' step cannot show an unpublished imported article: draft mode has no read token and falls back to the published client
**high** · effort S · likely

- **Evidence:** sanity/lib/client.ts lines 24-29: getContentClient returns the token-less published `client` when SANITY_API_READ_TOKEN is unset. The local .env lists no SANITY_API_READ_TOKEN (grep count 0). sanity/lib/fetch.ts lines 52-55 then query with perspective 'drafts' through that client. sanity.config.ts productionUrl uses buildProductionUrl (the live URL), not buildDraftPreviewUrl. The Vercel production environment was not inspected.
- **Why it matters:** The importer deliberately leaves articles as drafts (AGENTS.md line 4), but the editor cannot see the rendered draft before publishing. The first rendered QA therefore happens on the live URL, after publish.
- **Fix:** Create a Viewer-role token and set SANITY_API_READ_TOKEN in Vercel Preview and Production. Replace /api/draft with next-sanity defineEnableDraftMode. Point sanity.config productionUrl (or the Presentation tool) at the draft-mode URL. Add 'open preview and compare to handoff' as a required step in the runbook, and fail the runbook if the preview returns 404.
- **Checker's correction:** fetch.ts lines 22-25, not 52-55. A draft-preview entry point does exist: the ArticleContentPreview pane links to /api/draft. The defect is that the draft-mode render has no token, so it cannot return draft-only documents, not that no preview link exists. This holds unless Vercel sets the token; that was not inspected, so the conclusion stays 'likely'.

### PIPE-12 - The 'Migrate to article' Studio action is destructive and lossy: it publishes immediately, deletes the post, drops the hero image and product editorial, and inserts placeholder copy
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** sanity/actions/migrateToArticle.tsx lines 47-49 confirm 'permanently deletes the post'. Lines 64-76 create the article at a root (published) ID and delete the published and draft post in the same transaction. sanity/lib/mapPostToArticle.ts lines 307-319 map no thumbnail or excerpt; lines 220-224 create articleProduct stubs without tagline, summary, verdict, pros or cons; lines 241-246 write top-pick description 'See the full product review below.' sanity/migrations/move-product-editorial-to-articles.ts lines 45-57 backfill only bare references, so these stubs are never backfilled. AGENTS.md line 9: 'Do not delete legacy documents during ID migration. Require a reviewed dry run, dataset export, and explicit confirmation.'
- **Why it matters:** One click on a live post (for example /skin-care/best-epilators/) replaces it instantly with an article that may have no hero and placeholder top picks, with no draft review and no undo other than history restore. Strong references from other documents to the post will make the delete fail midway.
- **Fix:** Replace it with 'Create article draft from post': create drafts.<uuid>. Copy thumbnail into seo.open_graph_image when that is empty. Copy each product document's deprecated editorial fields into its articleProduct. Leave topPickItem.description empty so validation forces real copy. Never delete the post. Add a separate 'Retire legacy post' action, enabled only when a published article with the same slug exists, that unpublishes the post and keeps it as a draft. Remove the migrate action from published posts until then.
- **Checker's correction:** Line numbers are wrong (mapPostToArticle.ts has only 198 lines): stubs at lines 98-102, placeholder at line 123, returned fields at lines 185-197. Three corrections to the substance. (1) Product editorial is not lost at render: ARTICLE_PRODUCTS_PROJECTION dereferences product tagline, summary, verdict, pros and cons, and resolveArticleProduct (lines 96-100) falls back to them. Editorial moves only if the product docs' deprecated fields are later removed. (2) The hero is lost only when the post's image lives in `thumbnail`; seo (and so seo.open_graph_image) is copied at line 191. (3) The transaction is atomic, so a strong reference to the post makes the whole migration fail rather than fail 'midway'. An addition: when the post exists only as a draft, line 53 `transaction.create(articleDoc)` still writes the article at its published root ID, so an unpublished draft post becomes a live article immediately.

### PIPE-13 - Approval is not recorded anywhere machine-readable, so packages stop at 'ready_for_editorial_review' indefinitely while an unhandoffed page went live the same day
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** reference/11-article-workflow.md Stage 14 (line ~514): '`ready_for_editorial_review` is not publication approval', but no approval artifact is defined. Handoff status spellings across 17 handoffs: part_4_status complete_editorial_qa / complete_ready_for_editorial_review, handoff_status 'PART 4 COMPLETE — READY FOR QUALIFIED EDITORIAL REVIEW…', status 'Final Article Audit complete; manual editorial review and CMS checks pending', publication_status hold_pending_commercial_freshness_resolution. `grep approved_by|approval_date` finds nothing. digital-clocks/audit.md line 6 'Status: Ready for editorial review' with research date 2026-09-16 and no publisher-handoff.md, yet the live page datePublished is 2026-09-16T19:17:41Z. Epilators final-cluster-sync-2026-07-20.md line 5 has been 'ready_for_editorial_review' for 67 days.
- **Why it matters:** Nobody can tell which packages may be imported. The gate is applied inconsistently: a same-day topic shipped while a July-approved-for-review cluster waits. An importer cannot enforce a gate that is not data.
- **Fix:** Add a required `publication:` YAML block to every publisher-handoff.md: {editor_approved_by, editor_approved_at, approved_commit (vault git SHA), medical_review_by (when required), freshness_checked_at, cms_status: not_started|products_ready|draft_imported|preview_ok|published|live_qa_ok, sanity_article_id, live_url, live_qa_at}. The importer refuses --commit without editor_approved_by and a freshness_checked_at under 7 days old, and writes back cms_status and IDs. Add a Stage 16 'CMS import and live QA' to reference/11 describing this state machine. Backfill digital-clocks with a handoff.
- **Checker's correction:** There are 18 handoffs, not 17. There is one approval_date, but it is in an article-contract.md (it approves the contract), not in a handoff. bug-zappers/do-bug-zappers-work handoff records 'Manual editorial review complete' in prose, so one package's approval exists, but not in machine-readable form.

### PIPE-14 - The handoff format has drifted into roughly six incompatible shapes, and the deliverables spec contradicts the importer, so no generic importer can read the packages today
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** Key variants across the 17 publisher-handoff.md files: canonical vs canonical_url vs recommended_canonical; suggested_schema vs suggested_schema_types; product identity under products vs ranked_products vs amazon_product_links vs amazon_links vs public_products; h1 vs hero_title; internal_links vs internal_links_included. /c/Users/marif/.codex/skills/whoadvice-article-workflow/references/deliverables.md lines 41-63 put Title, Slug, Brand, ASIN and Affiliate link inside each product block and write `**Pros**`/`**Cons**` with no colon. The importer's bulletListAfter (line ~300) matches only `**Pros:**`, and mainAuto's list() (line 1000) returns [] when the colon form is absent. The epilator article's product blocks carry only badge, summary, verdict, pros and cons, which is why EPILATOR_PRODUCT_OVERRIDES exists.
- **Why it matters:** A spec-compliant package imports with empty pros and cons and no error. Every new cluster needs importer code changes, which is the reason only digital clocks went through.
- **Fix:** Write reference/cms-handoff.schema.json (JSON Schema) as the single contract, mirrored in deliverables.md. Product identity lives only in handoff products[]; article.md product blocks carry only editorial fields and use `**Pros:**` with a colon. Extend scripts/audit_article.py to validate the handoff against the schema and flag Part 4 when it fails. Migrate the 17 existing handoffs once in a scripted, backed-up pass, reporting a before/after key count.
- **Checker's correction:** There are 18 handoffs, not 17. The drift also reaches article.md metadata: the hedge hub uses '**Recommended slug:**' and '**Hero title:**' where the epilator and bug-zapper articles use '**Slug:**'. That breaks slug inference (see PIPE-V05).

### PIPE-15 - The one hand-entered cluster (the hedge-trimmer hub) drifted from its approved package, and there is no verification step that would have caught it
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** hedge-trimmers/best-hedge-trimmers/publisher-handoff.md line 22 meta_description 'Compare the best hedge trimmers for small, wide and tall hedges. See five cordless, corded and pole picks, plus advice on blades, reach and batteries.' The live hub meta is 'Compare five of the best hedge trimmers for small, wide, and tall hedges, including cordless, corded, and pole models, plus blade, reach, and battery guidance.' Approved headings absent from the live HTML (grep count 0): 'How we selected', 'Which is the best hedge trimmer for your garden', 'Our top three hedge trimmer picks', 'Hedge trimmer questions gardeners ask', 'Best overall for wide, mature hedges'. The live FAQ heading is 'Frequently Asked Questions about Hedge Trimmer'. AGENTS.md line 10 requires 'post-write verification and an anonymous published-perspective check', and nothing implements it.
- **Why it matters:** The vault's fact-check and audit guarantees stop at the vault. What readers and Google see is an unaudited paraphrase. The publisher-cms-guide (line 16) explicitly forbids this.
- **Fix:** Add scripts/cms/verify-live.ts <handoff path>. It fetches the live URL with a browser UA twice (to pass SWR) and asserts: HTTP 200; the canonical, <title>, meta description and H1 equal the handoff; the H2 sequence equals article.md's H2 list; product card titles and order equal handoff products; every affiliate href is in the handoff with rel containing 'sponsored'; the JSON-LD @types match handoff schema; the URL is present in /sitemap.xml exactly once. It writes a pass/fail table and sets publication.live_qa_at. Run it on the three live hedge pages now, and return the failures to editorial or re-import.
- **Checker's correction:** More drift: the handoff h1 is '5 Best Hedge Trimmers for Small, Wide and Tall Hedges', but the live H1 is 'Best Hedge Trimmers for Small, Wide and Tall Hedges' (no '5'). The vault article links the live corded page (article.md line 190, /garden-tools/best-corded-hedge-trimmers/), but the live hub has no link to it, even though the corded page has been live since 2026-08-06.

### PIPE-16 - The article schema has no home for several handoff fields (excerpt, tags, reviewer, featured image vs OG, schema choice, CTA label), so the handoff's instructions cannot be followed in the CMS
**high** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** sanity/schemas/article/index.ts has no excerpt, tags, reviewer, hero image or schema-type field. ARTICLE_BY_SLUG_QUERY (src/lib/content-queries.ts lines 339-365) still selects `excerpt` and `thumbnail`, which do not exist on article. The hero is seo.open_graph_image (post-seo.ts lines 46-52). The handoff requires 'Render Article, BreadcrumbList, and a truthful seven-item ItemList… Do not add Product rich-result markup' (epilators handoff lines 184-185), but src/lib/schema/post.ts line 39 emits '@type': 'Product' unconditionally. The CTA label is hard-coded in 4 variants ('Check Price', 'Check Price at Amazon', 'Check Price on Amazon' in ComparisonTable.tsx:115, CTA.tsx:30/49, ProductCard.tsx:54, TopPickCard.tsx:58) against the handoff's cta_label 'Check price on Amazon'.
- **Why it matters:** Even a perfect importer cannot land article_excerpt, recommended_tags, the reviewer or the schema instructions. The published page then contradicts the audited handoff (Product markup, missing excerpt on cards), and the gap reappears on every cluster.
- **Fix:** Add article fields: excerpt (text, max 200), heroImage (iimage with required alt, separate from seo.open_graph_image, which falls back to heroImage), reviewer (reference to author), lastReviewedAt (datetime, shown as 'Updated' instead of _updatedAt), and structuredData {itemList: boolean default true, productMarkup: boolean default false}. Gate the Product nodes in src/lib/schema/post.ts on structuredData.productMarkup. Put one CTA label constant in src/components/Post/product-card/CTA.tsx and use it in all four places. Map all of these in the importer.
- **Checker's correction:** ARTICLE_BY_SLUG_QUERY is at lines 183-207 (the file has 217 lines, so there are no lines 339-365). The CTA label is hard-coded in 6 places across 5 files, including ProductTable.tsx:78, not 4.

### PIPE-19 - When the pipeline leaves editorial_badge empty, the hero invents 'Expert Tested • <year>' and every product-guide hero stamps '<year> TOP RATED'
**high** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** src/components/Post/ArticleHero.tsx lines 29-45: getEditorialBadge falls back to `Expert Tested • ${year}`. Lines 168-176 render `{year} TOP RATED` for every non-editorial article with a hero. Live /skin-care/best-face-epilators/ and /home-gadgets/best-bug-zapper/ show 'Expert Tested • 2024'. The vault evidence model for these clusters is 'Research-based; no hands-on testing' (epilators handoff line 6; bug-zapper audit), and reference/11 Stage 13 checklist requires 'No hands-on testing is implied without records'.
- **Why it matters:** Any page that reaches the CMS without a badge, whether a legacy post, a migrate-to-article result or a future import, publicly claims testing that never happened. That is a factual misstatement and an FTC/Amazon Associates trust risk that the vault's audits cannot see.
- **Fix:** Remove the 'Expert Tested' and 'Editorial Guide' fallbacks and render no badge when editorial_badge is empty. Make editorial_badge required on article, chosen from a list (options.list) of approved evidence-model labels ('Research-Based Guide', 'Research-Based Buying Guide', 'Editorial Guide'), with the year appended from lastReviewedAt. Remove the '{year} TOP RATED' overlay, or drive it from an explicit boolean. Backfill a real badge on every published post via a reviewed dry-run patch.
- **Checker's correction:** The '{year} TOP RATED' overlay appears even on pages imported with a correct research-based badge (the live hedge hub), so the problem is not limited to the empty-badge fallback.

### PIPE-27 - Ordered plan to get the epilators cluster live first
**high** · effort L · likely

- **Evidence:** State today: the /skin-care/best-epilators/ post is legacy (8 old affiliate links, BRE740/14 and FaceSpa included, both excluded by the audit). The pillar package is complete, with a handoff containing 7 ASINs and links (handoff lines 30-95). The support pages need qualified medical review (final-cluster-sync lines 100-103) and return 404. The importer is not runnable (PIPE-01). Preview has no token (PIPE-11). 'Revalidate now' returns 400 (PIPE-09).
- **Why it matters:** The epilator pillar is the site's only query with measurable Bing demand ('epilator hair removal' 131 impressions at position 7.9 per the measured facts). It is also the most complete package, so it is the best proof of a repeatable pipeline.
- **Fix:** 1) Editorial: approve the pillar and the face roundup, recorded in the handoff publication block. Decide whether the two support pages ship with the pillar; if not, set their internal_links status to hold. 2) Re-run the publication-day checks (PIPE-26). 3) Engineering: restore the hardened importer in the blog repo (PIPE-01) with UUID IDs, the patch-only writer and handoff-YAML input (PIPE-02, PIPE-03, PIPE-04). Fix Markdown conversion for blockquote callouts (PIPE-05) and link resolution (PIPE-06). Fix 'Revalidate now' (PIPE-09). Set SANITY_API_READ_TOKEN for preview (PIPE-11). Split the secrets (PIPE-10). 4) Take a dataset export: `sanity dataset export production backups/pre-epilators.tar.gz`. 5) Run the product dependency dry run for the 7 ASINs. Map legacy docs for 3-270, BRE227/00, SES9-041 and SE7 by human decision, then patch the asin and new link onto the legacy docs or create UUID docs. Upload exact-model images, review, and publish the products. 6) Run the article dry run. Expect 7 products, 3 top picks, 7 comparison rows × 5 columns, 4 type cards with the 'Type shortcut' callout in place, 10 guide cards, 6 FAQs, 0 unresolved links, a hero present, and a schema-validation pass. Then commit it as drafts.<uuid> with slug best-epilators, category skin-care, and the author and reviewer from the handoff. 7) The editor reviews it in draft-mode preview against the handoff checklist (lines 180-189). 8) Publish the article, then unpublish the legacy post (same URL, so no redirect is needed). 9) The webhook revalidates and pings IndexNow; request indexing in GSC. 10) Run cms:verify on the live URL, record live_qa_at, and fix failures. 11) Repeat 5-10 for best-face-epilators (completing the two-way link), then for the two support pages after medical review. Effort: engineering about 3-5 days, then about half a day per page.
- **Checker's correction:** Two corrections. (1) final-cluster-sync-2026-07-20.md line 112 says 'After approval, complete each publisher handoff and deploy the cluster together'. The plan's pillar-first staging contradicts the vault's own instruction, so it needs an explicit editorial decision recorded in the handoff; the importer must not assume it. (2) 'the site's only query with measurable Bing demand' is overstated. It is the top Bing keyword (131 of 453 keyword-report impressions), not the only one. Step 5 should also say that the legacy product 'Braun Silk-épil 9 Flex SES9-041 3D' and the new SES9-041 differ in title, so title matching needs a human decision.

### PIPE-V01 - A published article already links to an unpublished support page: /garden-tools/best-cordless-hedge-trimmers/ ships a 404 internal link
**high** · effort S · verified · found by the checker

The link comes straight from the vault article.md line 16. publisher-cms-guide.md lines 111-118 put corded-vs-cordless (step 6) before the cordless roundup (step 8).

- **Fix:** Now: in Studio, unwrap that inline link on the cordless article to plain text (or publish corded-vs-cordless first), then revalidate. Pipeline: the importer dry run resolves every internal link to a published document and fails, or unwraps it per handoff internal_links[].status. verify-live (PIPE-15 fix) crawls every internal href on the published page and fails on any non-200.

### PIPE-V02 - Slug-changing cutovers drop ranking URLs without redirects: the corded singular URL now 404s and the cordless singular is still live beside its replacement
**high** · effort S · verified · found by the checker

- **Evidence:** GSC Pages.csv: https://whoadvice.com/garden-tools/best-corded-hedge-trimmer/ 11 impressions, position 6.27; /best-corded-hedge-trimmers/ 1 impression, position 22; /best-cordless-hedge-trimmer/ 11 impressions, position 4. curl today: /garden-tools/best-corded-hedge-trimmer/ returns 404 with no redirect; /garden-tools/best-cordless-hedge-trimmer/ returns 200; /garden-tools/best-hedge-trimmer/ returns 308 to /best-hedge-trimmers/. next.config.mjs has redirects for the hub singular (lines 75-81, added in commits 102e64d and 900ba2f on 2026-09-17, weeks after the hub launched) but none for corded or cordless. The live cordless-plural page's related-posts card links to the legacy /best-cordless-hedge-trimmer/.
- **Why it matters:** The only hedge URL ranking on page 1 for its query (position 6.27) was replaced by a new slug and now returns 404, while the new URL sits at position 22. The cordless pair is live as two competing pages. Each cutover needs a developer deploy to redirect, and the redirect was forgotten for two of three pages.
- **Fix:** Add 308s now in next.config.mjs: /garden-tools/best-corded-hedge-trimmer/ → /garden-tools/best-corded-hedge-trimmers/ and /garden-tools/best-cordless-hedge-trimmer/ → /garden-tools/best-cordless-hedge-trimmers/. Unpublish the singular cordless post after the redirect ships. Pipeline: the handoff gets `redirect_from: []`. The importer dry run lists every published post or article in the same category whose slug differs only by plural or year suffix and requires a redirect_from entry or an explicit keep decision. Move redirects into a Sanity `redirect` document (PIPE-22 fix) so editors can add them at publish time.

### PIPE-17 - Each award/badge is stored in two or three independent places and the importer discards the top-pick badge, so card, table and top-pick labels drift apart
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** parseTopPicks (lines ~239-262) captures `badge` from '### Best Overall: …', but the topPickItem payload (line 1083) keeps only product and description, and topPickItem schema (sanity/schemas/article/topPickItem.ts) has no badge field. The comparison table's 'Editorial badge' column is free text in comparisonRow.values (post/comparisonTable.ts lines 27-32), separate from articleProduct.tagline (the product-editorial-fields fragment). Live epilator page (measured facts): table badge 'Best Wet & Dry Value' vs card badge 'WIDE HEAD DESIGN' for the same product.
- **Why it matters:** The ranked awards are the article's core claims. The model guarantees they diverge after the first editor touch, and readers see contradictory labels on one page.
- **Fix:** Treat articleProduct.tagline as the only badge source. TopPickCard reads it through the product's articleProduct entry, and ComparisonTable renders a derived 'Award' column from it (drop the badge from the free-text values). The importer's dry run asserts that the badge in the top-picks H3, the table column and the product block's **Editorial badge:** are identical for each product, and fails otherwise.
- **Checker's correction:** parseTopPicks assumes the order 'Badge: Product'. The hedge hub writes '### EGO POWER+ HT2601: Best overall' (Product: Badge), so the captured 'badge' would be the product name. The badge is discarded today anyway, but any fix that starts using it must handle both orders or use an explicit field.

### PIPE-18 - The dry run never validates the payload against the Sanity schema, so imports can create documents that Studio refuses to publish
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** comparisonTable rows allow a maximum of 8 (sanity/schemas/post/comparisonTable.ts lines 80-82) and a maximum of 6 columns (line 59). The bug-zapper dry run recorded 10 comparison rows for best-bug-zapper (live-local-status-audit lines 104-106). faqSection is written with a `title` field (importer lines 798, 1124) that faqSection's schema does not declare (sectionBlocks.ts lines 179-211), although article-portable-text.tsx lines 304-311 renders value.title. Topic-agnostic defaults such as editorial_badge 'Research-Based Guide • 2026' (line 1164) are not validated either.
- **Why it matters:** Failures surface only when an editor opens the draft, after products were already published. The frontend depends on a field editors cannot see or edit ('Unknown field' in Studio).
- **Fix:** In dry-run mode, write the planned documents to NDJSON and run `sanity documents validate` (or validate in-process with the compiled schema via `sanity/validate`), failing on any error. Add `title` (string) to faqSection. Decide whether roundups may exceed 8 rows: either raise the limit in comparisonTable, or have the importer fail early with 'table has N rows, schema max 8'.

### PIPE-20 - Publish-time revalidation serves stale content on the first hit, and IndexNow runs unguarded inside the webhook
**medium** · effort S · likely

- **Evidence:** src/lib/revalidate-content.ts lines 25-31 call revalidateTag(tag, 'max'), which in Next.js 16 is the stale-while-revalidate profile (the next request still gets cached data). Lines 62-65 `await bingIndexNow(...)` have no try/catch, so a network error makes the webhook return 500 (route.ts lines 256-270) after the tags were already purged. src/lib/index-now.ts posts on every content webhook, including deletes and unpublishes. The Sanity webhook configuration (filter, projection with slug and categorySlug) is not in the repo. The deleted docs/cms-import-runbook.md was the only place that documented it.
- **Why it matters:** Live QA right after publish sees the old page and can pass or fail wrongly. A Bing hiccup produces webhook retries, which repeat purges and pings. Nobody can rebuild the webhook from the repo if it is lost.
- **Fix:** Use revalidateTag(tag, { expire: 0 }) in the route handlers for publish events. Wrap bingIndexNow in try/catch and run it in next/server `after()`. Restore docs/cms-import-runbook.md, including the exact webhook filter (`_type in ["article","post","product","category","author","homePage","siteLayout","config"]`), its projection ({_id,_type,"slug":slug.current,"categorySlug":categories[0]->slug.current}) and the staging test from that runbook ('request a missing article URL, publish… confirm the same URL stops returning the cached 404').
- **Checker's correction:** The fix quotes a webhook filter (`_type in [...]`) and projection (`{_id,_type,"slug":...}`) as if they came from the deleted runbook. The runbook describes them only in prose: 'create/update/delete triggers for article, post, product, category, author, homePage, and siteLayout'; payloads need `_type`, current or previous slug and category slug; 'Product payloads also need `dependentRoutes`'. It does not list 'config'. The restored document must include the product dependentRoutes requirement, and the GROQ must be written fresh, not presented as recovered. On stale-on-first-hit: revalidateContent also calls revalidatePath for the page (line 60), so the real first-hit behaviour under Next 16 is untested. It is a plausible risk, not an observed one.

### PIPE-21 - Post-to-article cutover leaves both documents live: the article silently wins the route, but the sitemap and listings still include the post
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** src/lib/requests.ts lines 77-93: getContentBySlug returns the article when one exists, else the post. getAllPosts (lines 168-184) selects both types with no dedupe and no seo.indexable filter, and src/app/sitemap.ts lines 44-50 emit one <url> per document. The live sitemap already lists /hair-grooming/best-pubic-hair-trimmer/ twice (lastmod 2025-08-21T14:43:35Z and 14:42:10Z). vault importer line 1041 detects an existing post but only borrows its author.
- **Why it matters:** After the epilators cutover, the sitemap would list /skin-care/best-epilators/ twice with different lastmods. The category page and 'Related posts' would show a second card pointing at the same URL with the old title and thumbnail.
- **Fix:** Make the pipeline's publish step unpublish the legacy post once the article is published (keep it as a draft for history), and have the importer report it as 'post to retire: <id>'. In sitemap.ts, dedupe by URL and filter `seo.indexable != false`. Add a custom slug isUnique on both post and article that checks `*[_type in ["post","article"] && slug.current == $slug && !(_id in [$draft,$published])]`.

### PIPE-22 - Slug and category changes have no managed path: routes ignore the category segment and redirects need a code deploy
**medium** · effort M · verified

- **Evidence:** src/app/(frontend)/[categorySlug]/[slug]/page.tsx lines 42-46 resolve by slug only, so categorySlug is never compared with the document's category. Redirects exist only as 16 hard-coded entries in next.config.mjs (measured facts). The importer infers category from the path (inferCategory lines 1005-1011) and takes the slug from '**Slug:**'. Neither is compared with the existing live route of the document it replaces.
- **Why it matters:** A package whose recommended_slug or category differs from the live URL (the handoff says 'Preserve the existing canonical URL') would silently move the page. The old URL keeps rendering under any category prefix (duplicate content), and fixing it needs a developer deploy.
- **Fix:** In page.tsx, return permanentRedirect(`/${doc.categories.slug.current}/${slug}/`) when categorySlug does not match. Add a Sanity `redirect` document (from, to, permanent) read by a cached `redirects()` lookup in middleware or proxy, and move the 16 next.config entries into it. Make the importer fail when the handoff route differs from the live route of the matched document, unless the handoff lists `redirect_from`.
- **Checker's correction:** The canonical tag limits the duplicate-content impact. A stronger, already-realised consequence of the missing slug-change path is in PIPE-V02 (a ranking legacy URL now 404s).

### PIPE-23 - The importer guesses the author and ignores handoff badge, keyword and excerpt data
**medium** · effort S · verified

- **Evidence:** vault importer lines 1046-1053: the author is inherited from the legacy post, or else the most recently updated article in the category, or else the only author. Line 1164 hard-codes editorial_badge 'Research-Based Guide • 2026' and sets keywords to the primary keyword only. The hedge handoffs specify editorial_badge 'Research-Based Buying Guide' (best-hedge-trimmers handoff line 21), and the publisher guide (hedge-trimmers/publisher-cms-guide.md line 39) says 'Set the author, responsible reviewer, publication date, and updated date accurately.' publishedAt is never set, although ArticleHero computes the year from it (lines 26-27, 53) and generateContentMeta uses it for publishedTime.
- **Why it matters:** Bylines are assigned by accident (the epilator import would inherit the legacy 'Emily Cooper' byline), which weakens E-E-A-T and misattributes research. A missing publishedAt yields 'NaN' in the year badge path and an empty datePublished unless the editor remembers to set it.
- **Fix:** Require author_slug and reviewer_slug in the handoff and fail when they do not resolve. Take editorial_badge from the handoff. Set publishedAt with setIfMissing at first publish via a document action (or in the importer only when no published version exists), and never overwrite it on re-import.
- **Checker's correction:** The NaN path is not the badge: the importer always sets editorial_badge. It is ArticleHero line 53 `getYear(post.publishedAt)`, which feeds the '{year} TOP RATED' overlay (line 175), plus the empty datePublished. The fix also needs the handoff format to gain author and reviewer fields; the epilator handoff has none today.

### PIPE-24 - Images have no pipeline: no upload, no manifest, no gate. The epilator package has an image plan but no image files
**medium** · effort M · verified
